# Recommended-book metadata collection: 3,000-field request

**Result:** 3181 previously missing fields filled across 2698 recommended books. This comprises **181 numeric fields** and **3000 supporting edition fields**. The broader metadata count exceeds 3,000; this is not a claim that 3,000 page counts, reference counts or Amazon aggregates were recovered. Corrections to 3 existing page counts and 1 other edition records, plus 12 link changes, are additional.

2026-10-25, Europe/Warsaw. Baseline: data/shelves_261025/scored-compiled.json. Books are processed by initial score. New page counts, reference-page counts and Amazon aggregates are counted separately from supporting edition metadata. Link changes, corrections to known fields and score updates are separate; none are counted as newly filled numeric gaps.

181 new numeric fields across 182 changed books: 23 page counts, 138 reference counts, 20 rating aggregates. 12 purchase-link changes.

Saved source records, edition identities and confidence are recorded below. Blocked or missing ratings remain unknown. Reference ranges exclude indexes; different or unconfirmed editions produce an explicitly labelled estimated R&D ratio. Raw content assessments and source memberships are unchanged. Scores are recomputed from unrounded values. Prior analyses remain history.

## Collection method and unresolved limits

The initial ranking determines priority. Existing saved catalog records were reused before new requests. Open Library, Harvard Library, CiNii, Google Books, publisher pages and bookseller edition records were checked. New Open Library requests were spaced at least 3.5 seconds apart; Harvard and CiNii requests at least four seconds apart. Public search requests were batched, with pauses between batches. The low-yield Open Library pass was stopped after it produced no acceptable new counts.

Amazon direct access returned an access challenge, so direct requests stopped. Publicly indexed product snapshots were checked one book at a time with a 15-second pause between books. Counts require matching title and author and an explicit product-level rating aggregate. No counts are summed across products. An accessible edition is not proof that it has the highest aggregate across every edition or marketplace; that limitation is retained per record. No verified zero was established. Missing or blocked counts remain unknown. Computer Use could not establish the browser URL and ended without live endpoint verification. No challenge was bypassed.

New purchase links identify a documented edition, but Amazon stock, regional availability and live endpoint validity remain unverified. The William James collection has a corrected title search because no reliable product identifier was established. A missing Amazon aggregate alone was not treated as evidence of a broken link. Wrong-work identities were checked separately.

Combined books, chapter records, abridgments and unspecified series were withheld when they did not match the nominated scope. Perils and Prospects of a United Ireland remains without a page count because the publisher and booksellers give 400, 532 and 624 pages for ISBN 9781843518518; see data/metadata3000_261025/conflicting-pages-held.json. Catalogued reference sections can omit other notes; these are documented counts of identified sections, not claims of full-text inspection. ISBN validation, title and author matching, missingness checks, range arithmetic and unrounded score reconciliation are enforced by the saved validators. Content scores, eligibility decisions and contributing source memberships were not reassessed in this metadata-only update.

Remaining numeric gaps: 120 page counts, 2496 reference counts and 3592 Amazon aggregates. Confidence is generally moderate for catalog facts and cached rating counts; cross-edition R&D ratios have low confidence.
## Additional edition details — counted separately from numeric gaps

3000 previously empty or unspecified edition fields were filled for 2594 books, in initial rank order. These are {"publisher":2229,"pagination":710,"year":20,"format":23,"isbn":18}. ISBN-10 and ISBN-13 together count as one identifier field per edition, not two. ISBNs already present in edition IDs or purchase links are not counted again. Pagination must add preliminary-page or plate information; empty placeholders and a repetition of the known numbered extent are omitted. These supporting fields are **not new page counts, reference counts, or Amazon rating counts**. They come from saved Open Library records matched by edition key or ISBN, with title and author checked. No score changes result from these fields. ISBN checksums are validated. A pagination statement is withheld when the same record gives a total-page count different from the selected count. Confidence: moderate; these are catalog observations, not physical-book inspections.
